The answer was: 'When form overcomes color or color overcomes form, upon reaching the season of dominance and the added year, harm brings illness and loss brings worry. When form and color harmonize with each other, there is prosperity and great joy.' Another asked: 'Can the time when form and color overcome each other and the added years be known?' The answer was: 'In general, major cautions for a person often add nine years. Ages seven, sixteen, twenty-five, thirty-four, forty-three, fifty-two, and sixty-one are all cautions for a person; one must not fail to keep oneself settled. Being affected brings illness, and losing balance brings worry.'

💡 【术语】形胜色、色胜形：望诊中形体特征与面色光泽不相匹配或偏胜；年加：岁运或生命年龄周期推演。【医理】古人认为形与色相得为顺，形色失调、逢时令气交或特定年龄节点易诱发疾病或情志波动。【适用与现代对照】古代运气与生命周期观，现代医学不承认固定的“某岁必病”宿命论，但可参考青春期、青壮年、中年及更年期等生理/心理转折期的健康管理与预防保健。

曰︰脈之上下血氣之候，以知形氣奈何？曰︰足陽明之上，血氣盛則須美長，血多氣少則須短，氣多血少則須少，血氣俱少則無須，兩吻多畫。（須字一本俱作髯字，吻音穩。）足陽明之下，血氣盛則下毛美長至胸；血多氣少則下毛美短至臍，行則善高舉足，足大指少肉，足善寒，血少氣多則肉善瘃（瘃音）；血氣皆少則無毛，有則稀而枯瘁，善痿厥足痺。足少陽之上，血氣盛則通髯美長，血多氣少則通髯美短，血少氣多則少髯，血氣皆少則無髯，感於寒濕，則善痺骨痛爪枯。足少陽之下，血氣盛則脛毛美長，外踝肥；血多氣少則脛毛美短，外踝皮堅而濃；血少氣多則毛少，外踝皮薄而軟；血氣皆少則無毛，外踝瘦而無肉。足太陽之上，血氣盛則美眉，眉有毫毛；血多氣少則惡眉，面多小理；血少氣盛則面多肉，血氣和則美色。足太陰之下，血氣盛則跟肉滿，踵堅；氣少血多則瘦，跟空；血氣皆少則善轉筋，踵下痛。手陽明之上，氣血盛則上髭美，血少氣多則髭惡，血氣皆少則善轉筋，無髭。手陽明之下，血氣盛則腋下毛美，手魚肉以溫；氣血皆少則手瘦以寒。手少陽之上，血氣盛則眉美以長，耳色美；血氣皆少則耳焦惡色。手少陽之下，血氣盛則手拳多肉以溫；血氣皆少則瘦以寒；氣少血多則瘦以多脈。手太陽之上，血氣盛則多髯，面多肉以平；血氣皆少則面瘦黑色。手太陽之下，血氣盛則掌肉充滿；血氣皆少則掌瘦以寒。

问道：通过经脉上下部位的血气征象，来了解形体与气血状况的方法如何？回答说：足阳明经上部，血气盛则胡须润泽而长（一本作“髯”），血多气少则须短，气多血少则须少，血气俱少则无须，口角周围纹路较多（吻，音wěn）。足阳明经下部，血气盛则下体体毛润泽长及胸；血多气少则下体体毛较短及脐，行走时喜欢高抬足，足大趾肌肉较少，足部易发凉，血少气多则肌肉易生冻疮（瘃，音zhú）；血气皆少则无毛，即使有也稀疏枯槁，易出现痿软、厥冷、足部麻木。足少阳经上部，血气盛则连腮胡须润泽而长，血多气少则连腮须较短，血少气多则须少，血气皆少则无须，感受寒湿则易患痹证、骨痛、爪甲枯槁。足少阳经下部，血气盛则小腿毛润泽长，外踝丰满；血多气少则小腿毛较短，外踝皮肤坚厚；血少气多则毛少，外踝皮肤薄软；血气皆少则无毛，外踝消瘦无肉。足太阳经上部，血气盛则眉毛美观，眉中可有毫毛；血多气少则眉毛不佳、面部细纹多；血少气盛则面部肌肉丰满，血气平和则面色润泽美观。足太阴经下部，血气盛则足跟肌肉丰满、足跟坚实；气少血多则消瘦、足跟空虚；血气皆少则易抽筋（转筋）、足跟底痛。手阳明经上部，气血盛则上唇须（髭）美观，血少气多则须差，血气皆少则易抽筋、无须。手阳明经下部，血气盛则腋毛润泽、大鱼际肌肉丰满温热；气血皆少则手部消瘦发凉。手少阳经上部，血气盛则眉毛美观且长，耳部色泽润泽；血气皆少则耳部枯焦、面色晦暗。手少阳经下部，血气盛则握拳时手部肌肉丰满温热；气血皆少则消瘦发凉；气少血多则消瘦且脉络显露较多。手太阳经上部，血气盛则胡须多、面部肌肉丰满平整；血气皆少则面容消瘦色黑。手太阳经下部，血气盛则手掌肌肉充实；血气皆少则手掌消瘦发凉。

Someone asked: 'How does one know bodily form and Qi by observing Qi and blood in the upper and lower portions of the channels?' The answer was: 'In the upper Foot Yang Ming, abundant Qi and blood produce lush, long beards (one edition reads ran/whiskers); much blood and little Qi produce short beards; much Qi and little blood produce sparse beards; deficiency of both yields no beard, with more lines around the corners of the mouth (wen). In the lower Foot Yang Ming, abundant Qi and blood produce lush lower body hair reaching the chest; much blood and little Qi produce shorter hair reaching the navel, a tendency to lift the foot high when walking, less flesh on the big toe, and cold feet; little blood and much Qi predispose to frostbite/chilblains (zhu); deficiency of both yields no hair, or sparse, withered hair, with proneness to flaccidity/atrophy (wei), cold extremities (jue), and foot numbness. In the upper Foot Shao Yang, abundant Qi and blood produce lush, long full beards/whiskers; much blood and little Qi produce shorter ones; little blood and much Qi produce sparse ones; deficiency of both yields no whiskers, and exposure to cold-damp easily causes Bi-syndrome, bone pain, and brittle/withered nails. In the lower Foot Shao Yang, abundant Qi and blood produce lush, long shin hair and a full lateral malleolus; much blood and little Qi produce shorter shin hair and thick, firm skin over the lateral malleolus; little blood and much Qi produce sparse hair and thin, soft skin there; deficiency of both yields no hair and a lean, fleshless lateral malleolus. In the upper Foot Tai Yang, abundant Qi and blood produce fine eyebrows with long/fine hairs; much blood and little Qi produce poor eyebrows and fine facial lines; little blood and much Qi produce a fleshy face, and harmonious Qi and blood yield a fine complexion. In the lower Foot Tai Yin, abundant Qi and blood produce a full heel and firm calcaneal region; little Qi and much blood produce leanness and an empty heel; deficiency of both predisposes to cramping/spasms (zhuan jin) and pain under the heel. In the upper Hand Yang Ming, abundant Qi and blood produce fine upper-lip hair/mustache (zi); little blood and much Qi produce poor mustache; deficiency of both predisposes to cramping and no mustache. In the lower Hand Yang Ming, abundant Qi and blood produce fine axillary hair and a warm, fleshy thenar eminence (hand fish); deficiency of Qi and blood produces thin, cold hands. In the upper Hand Shao Yang, abundant Qi and blood produce long, fine eyebrows and a good ear complexion; deficiency of both produces scorched/withered ears and a dull complexion. In the lower Hand Shao Yang, abundant Qi and blood produce a warm, fleshy clenched fist; deficiency of Qi and blood produces leanness and coldness; little Qi and much blood produce leanness with prominent vessels. In the upper Hand Tai Yang, abundant Qi and blood produce abundant whiskers and a full, smooth face; deficiency of both produces a lean, dark face. In the lower Hand Tai Yang, abundant Qi and blood produce full palm flesh; deficiency of both produces thin, cold palms.'

💡 【术语】须/髯/髭：口周及面颊毛发之别称；瘃：冻疮；转筋：肌肉痉挛、抽筋；手鱼：手掌大鱼际部。【医理】通过观察十二经脉上下循行部位的毛发、肌肉、皮温、色泽及筋脉状态，判断该经气血盛衰，是“经络诊察法”的典范。【涉及穴位】足阳明（足三里 ST36、丰隆 ST40、厉兑 ST45）；足少阳（风池 GB20、阳陵泉 GB34、悬钟 GB39）；足太阳（攒竹 BL2、委中 BL40、昆仑 BL60）；足太阴（太白 SP3、三阴交 SP6）；手阳明（合谷 LI4、曲池 LI11）；手少阳（外关 TE5、丝竹空 TE23）；手太阳（后溪 SI3、养老 SI6）。【适用与现代对照】毛发分布、肢端皮温、肌肉丰满度确实受外周循环、激素及营养状态影响，可作一般观察；但用某处毛发长短判断某条经脉气血多少，属于古代诊察模型，现代不作诊断依据。

少陽多血，美須者陽明多血，此其時然也。夫人之常數，太陽常多血少氣，少陽常多氣少血，陽明常多血多氣，厥陰常多氣少血，少陰常多血少氣，太陰常多血少氣，此天之常數也。

少阳多血，胡须润泽美观者往往阳明血气较盛，这是相应时气与体质的表现。至于人体经脉气血的普遍规律，则是：太阳经常多血少气，少阳经常多气少血，阳明经常多血多气，厥阴经常多气少血，少阴经常多血少气，太阴经常多血少气，这就是自然禀赋的常理。

Shao Yang has abundant blood, and those with fine beards have abundant blood in Yang Ming—this is how it occurs in its time. As for the general constant pattern in humans: Tai Yang regularly has much blood and little Qi; Shao Yang regularly has much Qi and little blood; Yang Ming regularly has much blood and much Qi; Jue Yin regularly has much Qi and little blood; Shao Yin regularly has much blood and little Qi; and Tai Yin regularly has much blood and little Qi. This is the constant law of nature.

💡 【术语】多血少气、多气少血、多血多气：六经气血多少之常度（与《素问》《灵枢》部分篇目在少阳/少阴等表述上存在文献异文，需注意互参）。【医理】提出六经气血各有常度，阳明多气多血，故气血生化充盛；太阳、少阴等则各有偏重。【适用与现代对照】中医基础理论与针灸选穴重要参考（如阳明经穴常用于调理气血、补益正气，如足三里 ST36、合谷 LI4）；现代医学中无“经脉血气多少”的直接对应，但可理解为不同经络循行区血管与神经分布及功能负荷的差异。

曰︰二十五人者，刺之有約乎？曰︰美眉者，足太陽之脈血氣多；惡眉者，血氣少。其肥而澤者，血氣有餘；肥而不澤者，氣有餘，血不足。瘦而無澤者，血氣俱不足。審察其形氣經絡之凝泣結而不通者，此於身背為痛痺，甚則不行故凝泣，凝泣者致氣以溫之，血和乃止。其結絡者，脈結血不行，決之乃行。故曰︰氣有餘於上者，導而下之；氣不足於上者，推而往之；其稽留不至者，因而迎之。必明於經隧，乃能持之。寒與熱爭者，導而行之；其宛陳血不結者，即而取之。必先明知二十五人，別血氣之所在，左右上下，則刺約畢矣。

问道：对于这二十五种人，针刺治疗有什么要领或规律吗？回答说：眉毛润泽美观的，说明足太阳经脉血气较多；眉毛枯恶不佳的，说明血气较少。形体肥胖且润泽有光的，是血气有余；肥胖但缺乏光泽的，是气有余而血不足；形体消瘦且无光泽的，是血气都不足。仔细审察形体、气血与经络中凝滞结聚而不通畅的部位，这种状况在身背往往表现为痛痹，严重时气血完全不通而凝滞，对凝滞不通者应当引导气机以温通之，待血气和畅病痛才止。对于络脉结聚的，是脉络结滞、血行不畅，应当疏通决开使之运行。所以说：气有余于上部的，就引导它下行；气不足于上部的，就推动它向所往之处行进；气血滞留不至的，就顺势迎而导之；必须通晓经脉通道，才能把握施治。寒邪与热邪相争的，就引导气血宣通运行；对于郁滞未成块结的陈血，就直接刺而取之。必须先明确认识二十五种人的特征，辨别血气所在的左右、上下部位，那么针刺的要领就齐备了。

Someone asked: 'Is there a rule/guideline for needling these twenty-five types of people?' The answer was: 'Those with fine eyebrows have abundant Qi and blood in the Foot Tai Yang channel; those with poor eyebrows have scant Qi and blood. Those who are plump and lustrous have excess Qi and blood; those who are plump yet lack luster have excess Qi and insufficient blood; those who are thin and lack luster have deficiency of both Qi and blood. Carefully examine areas where form, Qi, and channels are coagulated, stagnant, and obstructed; on the back of the body this manifests as painful Bi-syndrome (Tong Bi), and when severe, non-flow causes stagnation. For stagnation, draw Qi to warm it, and it stops only when blood harmonizes. For knotted collaterals where blood does not flow, open/release them so flow resumes. Thus it is said: when Qi is excessive above, guide it downward; when Qi is deficient above, push it onward to where it should go; when Qi is delayed and fails to arrive, meet and guide it accordingly. One must be clear on the channel pathways to manage them. When cold and heat contend, guide and move the Qi; for stagnant blood that is not knotted, directly bleed/extract it. One must first clearly understand the twenty-five types and distinguish where Qi and blood lie—left or right, upper or lower—and then the guidelines for needling are complete.'

💡 【术语】痛痺：以剧烈疼痛为主的痹证；凝泣：气血凝滞涩行；宛陈血：瘀滞的陈血；刺约：针刺治疗的要领与法则。【医理】集望形察色、辨虚实、审经络凝结与针刺补泻、导引、刺络放血法则于一体，突出“先明形气经络，再定调治之法”。【涉及穴位】身背痛痹常取足太阳经背俞穴及夹脊穴（如大杼 BL11、风门 BL12、肺俞 BL13、肾俞 BL23、委中 BL40）；刺络放血可选委中 BL40 等。【适用与现代对照】“气有余导而下之”“结络决之”“宛陈血取之”是古代针刺调气与刺络的原则；现代有人拿它与肌筋膜激痛点处理作对照理解，但两者理论不同；放血须严格掌握适应证与无菌操作。

曰︰或神動而氣先針行，或氣與針相逢，或針已出氣獨行，或數刺之乃知，或發針而氣逆，或數刺病益甚。凡此六者，各不同形，願聞其方？曰︰重陽之盛人，其神易動，其氣易往也，矯矯蒿蒿（一本作高高），言語善疾，舉足喜高，心肺之臟氣有餘，陽氣滑盛而揚，故神動而氣先行，此人頗有陰者也。多陽者多喜，多陰者多怒，數怒者易解，故曰頗有陰。其陰陽之離合難，故其神不能先行。陰陽和調者，血氣淖澤滑利，故針入而氣出，疾而相逢也。其陰多而陽少，陰氣沉而陽氣浮者內藏，故針已出，氣乃隨其後，故獨行也。其多陰而少陽者，其氣沉而氣往難，故數刺之乃知。其氣逆與其數刺病益甚者，非陰陽之氣也，沉浮之勢也，此皆粗之所敗，工之所失，其形氣無過也。

问道：有的人心神一动而气机先于针刺行进，有的人气至与针刺相遇，有的人针已起出而气机才独自循行，有的人多次针刺后才察觉气至，有的人出针后出现气机逆乱，有的人多次针刺反而病情加重。凡是这六种情况，各自对应不同的形气表现，希望能听听其中的道理与方法？回答说：阳气极盛之人，其心神容易发动，气机容易趋往，举止昂扬高亢（一本作“高高”），说话很快，抬脚喜欢迈得高，心肺脏气有余，阳气滑利旺盛而宣扬，所以心神一动气就先行，这类人也还多少带有一些阴气（原文此处“此人颇有阴者也”至“故其神不能先行”存在古籍传抄错简/混入，大意谓：阳偏多者易喜，阴偏多者易怒，常怒而易解者说明尚有阴和之气；若阴阳离合不畅，则神气难顺畅先行）。阴阳调和之人，血气润泽滑利，所以针刺入而气应出，迅速与针相逢。阴多阳少、阴气沉伏而阳气浮越内藏之人，针已起出，气才随后循行，所以表现为气独行。阴多而少阳之人，其气沉降而运行较难，所以需要多次针刺才能觉察气至。至于出针后气逆，以及多次针刺后病情反而加重的，并非单纯阴阳之气所致，而是违背了气机升降沉浮之势；这些都是粗疏医者操作不当、医工失误造成的，并非其形气本身的过错。

Someone asked: 'Sometimes spirit/mind moves and Qi travels ahead of the needle; sometimes Qi and the needle meet; sometimes the needle is withdrawn and Qi travels on its own; sometimes it is perceived only after repeated needling; sometimes Qi rebels upon withdrawing the needle; and sometimes repeated needling makes the illness worse. All six of these present different forms—I wish to hear their principles and methods.' The answer was: 'In a person of abundant Double Yang, their spirit easily stirs and their Qi easily moves forth; they are lofty and vigorous (one edition reads high/tall), speak swiftly, like to lift their feet high, have excess Qi in the Heart and Lung, and have smooth, abundant, ascending Yang Qi—thus spirit stirs and Qi precedes the needle. Regarding those who have a fair share of Yin: those with more Yang tend to be joyful, those with more Yin tend to be angry, and those who get angry frequently yet resolve quickly have some Yin; when Yin and Yang have difficulty separating and joining, spirit cannot move ahead smoothly. In those whose Yin and Yang are harmonious, blood and Qi are moist, smooth, and fluent, so when the needle enters and Qi emerges, they meet swiftly. In those with more Yin and less Yang, where Yin Qi sinks and Yang Qi floats/is stored inward, Qi follows only after the needle is withdrawn, hence it travels alone. In those with much Yin and less Shao Yang, their Qi sinks and moves with difficulty, so perception comes only after repeated needling. As for Qi rebelling upon needle withdrawal and illness worsening after repeated needling, these are not due to Yin and Yang Qi alone, but to going against the momentum of sinking and floating; these are failures of the crude practitioner and errors of the clinician, not faults of the patient's form and Qi.'

💡 【术语】神动而气先针行：得气与气行感应较快；气与针相逢：进针即得气；针已出气独行：留针或出针后气感延续；数刺之乃知：多次针刺方得气；气逆：气机逆乱；沉浮之势：气机升降沉浮的顺逆态势。【医理】将“得气快慢、气行迟速”与体质阴阳虚实结合，同时明确指出“气逆”与“屡刺病甚”多属医者手法或辨证失误（如违背气机沉浮、补泻不当），体现高度的临床反思精神。【适用与现代对照】针灸“得气”与个体敏感度相关，现代可联系神经兴奋性、痛阈及自主神经反应差异；对“粗之所败，工之所失”的强调在针灸教学与临床质控中极为重要，提醒临床医生遇到气逆或病情加重时应先检视操作与辨证，切忌一味归咎患者。
